There is no safety in stagnation. I would rather face the storm than wither away in this... this mausoleum of love.\"\n\nHe looked at me then, his eyes reflecting a maelstrom of emotions. \"You are determined, then?\"\n\n\"I am,\" I replied, my voice as firm as the first toll of a funeral bell.\n\nHe nodded, a slow, resigned motion. \"Then go, Jane.
